## Scope

This is **not** a per-checkpoint review — D1..D5 have each PASSed their own Codex rounds.
This is the **formal D phase gate**: a cross-D cumulative audit. Verify the FULL authority
chain and semantic invariants hold END-TO-END across the merged D1..D5 surface.

Be TERSE. For each finding output ONE block:

Finding D-GATE-NN: <one-line summary>

  proposed closure: <change>

After ALL findings, end with EXACTLY one line:

or

FINAL VERDICT: BLOCK

## Cross-D invariants to verify

### Authority chain (full D1 → D5 lineage)

1. D1 contract layer (contracts/r3.0d/*) enforces closed key sets + mandatory presence rules + no causal overclaim.
2. D2 EvidenceGraph: closure-private WeakSet + `verifyAuthoritativeGraph` (per its Codex R20 closure). Cross-binding: graphId FNV-1a recompute; node sanitization via D1.
3. D3 HypothesisSet: closure-private WeakSet + `verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet`. Cross-binding: sourceGraphId.
4. D4 PrioritySet: closure-private WeakSet + `verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet`. Cross-binding: sourceHypothesisSetId + sourceGraphId.
5. D5 EngineerBrief: closure-private WeakSet + `verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief`. Authority chain HE → PE → cross-binding → identity.
6. D5 ViewModel + Orchestrator: closure-private WeakSet + `verifyAuthoritativeEngineerViewModel`. Subscriber snapshots are NOT in the WeakSet.

For each WeakSet boundary: captured `Reflect.apply` on captured `WeakSet.prototype.{add,has}`; rebinding `.has = () => true` AND `.add = throw` does not corrupt verification.

### Hardened intrinsics (HI)

7. All ambient method calls in renderer/js/r3-0d-*.js (D2..D5 production) go through HI. No ambient `.every` / `.forEach` / `.push` / `Object.freeze` / `Array.isArray` / `JSON.stringify`.
8. Module-init captures of `Object.isFrozen`, `Date.parse`, `Object.is`, `WeakSet.prototype.*`, `Reflect.apply` are in place and used.

### Credibility / confidence / contradictions / cannotConclude (D1-enforced, D5-rendered)

9. Credibility ladder (`Physics` / `Model` / `Measured` / `Derived` / `Heuristic` / `Unavailable`) used consistently from D2 evidence-node credibility through D3 hypothesis credibility through D5 brief credibility. No laundering (D5 cannot upgrade D3's credibility).
10. Confidence is `{ state }` at D1 brief contract layer (no numeric score in brief). D5 renders as opaque enum, never numeric.
11. Contradictions preserved from D2 contradicting edges → D3 hypothesis contradictingEvidenceIds → D5 brief contradictions[]. Never hidden.
12. cannotConcludeReasonCodes preserved from D3 union across hypotheses → D5 brief cannotConcludeReasonCodes[]. Never hidden.
13. alternativeExplanations preserved similarly.

### Case / session / lap boundaries

14. caseAssociation + sessionAssociation propagate through D2 (graph) → D3 (hypothesisSet) → D4 (prioritySet) → D5 (brief.identity). Any cross-binding mismatch (e.g., D4 from case A paired with D3 from case B) rejected.
15. invalidate() across 13 reasons clears authoritative state.
16. Imported summary opens MUST block D5 brief generation.

### Privacy

17. No raw telemetry samples in any envelope.
18. No file paths / filenames in serialized brief / viewmodel / subscriber snapshot.
19. No machine IDs or session secrets.
20. No stack traces in reasonCodes or detail strings.

### UI (D5 only)

21. Render-only: no recomputation; no innerHTML / outerHTML / insertAdjacentHTML.
22. i18n keys only; tri-locale parity (en + zh-TW + ja); forbidden phrases absent.
23. Closed displayState enum.

### Governance

24. Train state.json + train.json + per-phase manifests consistent (D5_ENGINEER_BRIEF_ACTIVATION, finalActivationReached=true).
25. authorizedProductionPaths exact (10 R3.0D paths under Train; no E or F production paths).
26. R3.0E + R3.0F remain unstarted (E0_BOOTSTRAP / F0_BOOTSTRAP; uiAllowed=false; featureRegistryActivationAllowed=false).

### Bundle / browser surface

27. renderer/js/r3-0d-contracts-bundle.js generated by tools/build-r3-0d-browser-bundle.js; no literal `require(p)` substring; no `import(` substring.
28. index.html loads each D2..D5 production module exactly once (no duplicate instance).
29. Auto-mount script swallows errors; doesn't break host page if R3.0D modules are absent.

### Mutation test effectiveness (D2..D5 producer-attestation chain)

30. Every WeakSet boundary has tests proving the captured-method dispatch defeats prototype rebind.
31. Every cross-binding has tests with mismatched sources rejected.
32. Every authority gate is verifier-first (Proxy trap count = 0 on rejection).

If a finding is materially low-priority you may emit it as NIT, but err on the side of BLOCK
for any genuinely missing invariant. Maximum 12 findings; expected count is 0 since each
checkpoint has been independently verified.

End with FINAL VERDICT line. Nothing else after it.

    90	  function _isoToMs(s) {

   102	  var _WeakSetCtor = WeakSet;

   118	  function verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief(candidate) {
   119	    try {

   150	  function _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock(detail) {
   151	    var reasonsArr = HI.safeArraySlice([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION]);
   152	    var explanationsObj = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();

   154	    HI.safeDefineDataProperty(explanationsObj, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION, explKey);

   173	    catch (e) { return 0; }

   301	  function buildEngineerBrief(inputIn, optsIn) {
   302	    try {
   303	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   304	        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected at entry');
   305	      }
   306	
   307	      // ---- Step 1 — input descriptor snapshot --------------------------------------------------
   308	      var inSnapR = _snapshotInputDescriptors(inputIn);
   309	      if (inSnapR.valid !== true) {
   310	        return RC.buildBlockedResult(inSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: inSnapR.detail });
   311	      }
   312	      var inputSnap = inSnapR.snapshot;
   313	
   314	      // ---- Step 2 — D3 PRODUCER AUTHORITY (first read) -----------------------------------------
   315	      // D4 R5-01 closure: verifier MUST be the first read on caller-supplied data. The verifier
   316	      // itself does NOT fire Proxy traps for non-members (WeakSet.has via captured Reflect.apply).
   317	      if (typeof HE.verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet !== 'function'
   318	          || HE.verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet(inputSnap.hypothesisSet) !== true) {
   319	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'D3 hypothesisSet authority verification failed' });
   320	      }
   321	      var hs = inputSnap.hypothesisSet;
   322	
   323	      // ---- Step 3 — D4 PRODUCER AUTHORITY ------------------------------------------------------
   324	      if (typeof PE.verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet !== 'function'
   325	          || PE.verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet(inputSnap.prioritySet) !== true) {
   326	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'D4 prioritySet authority verification failed' });
   327	      }
   328	      var ps = inputSnap.prioritySet;
   329	
   330	      // ---- Step 4 — cross-binding --------------------------------------------------------------
   331	      // The D4 PrioritySet MUST have been derived from THIS D3 HypothesisSet.
   332	      if (ps.sourceHypothesisSetId !== hs.hypothesisSetId) {
   333	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'prioritySet not derived from this hypothesisSet' });
   334	      }
   335	      if (ps.sourceGraphId !== hs.sourceGraphId) {
   336	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'sourceGraphId mismatch between D3 and D4' });
   337	      }
   338	
   339	      // ---- Step 5 — identity binding -----------------------------------------------------------
   340	      var hsCA = hs.caseAssociation;

   342	      if (!_isPlainObject(hsCA) || !_isPlainObject(psCA)) {
   343	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID], { detail: 'caseAssociation shape invalid' });
   344	      }
   345	      if (hsCA.caseId !== psCA.caseId || hsCA.sessionId !== psCA.sessionId || hsCA.lapId !== psCA.lapId) {
   346	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID], { detail: 'caseAssociation mismatch between D3 and D4' });
   347	      }

   353	      }

   359	      }

   361

    65	function stripDynamicRequireForBrowser(src) {
    66	  // Replace every literal "require(p)" with the literal "null". This affects
    67	  // ONLY the bundle copy; the Node-side contracts/r3.0d/* sources are unmodified.
    68	  var out = src.replace(/require\(p\)/g, 'null');
    69	  return out;
    70	}

    72	function buildBundle() {
    73	  var header = '// renderer/js/r3-0d-contracts-bundle.js — GENERATED, DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.\n'
    74	    + '// Concatenation of contracts/r3.0d/* in dependency order for browser use.\n'
    75	    + '// Node test harness loads contracts/r3.0d/* directly; this bundle is browser-only.\n'
    76	    + '// Regenerate via:  node tools/build-r3-0d-browser-bundle.js\n';
    77	  var pieces = [header];
    78	  for (var i = 0; i < ORDER.length; i++) {
    79	    var src = fs.readFileSync(path.join(IN_DIR, ORDER[i]), 'utf8');
    80	    var transformed = stripDynamicRequireForBrowser(src);
    81	    pieces.push('\n// ====== contracts/r3.0d/' + ORDER[i] + ' ======\n');
    82	    pieces.push(transformed);
    83	  }
    84	  return pieces.join('');
    85	}
